Each output element accumulates k in the original\n// order; the innermost loop vectorizes across independent output columns.\nvoid multiplyPanel(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,\n                   size_t rows, size_t cols, size_t width) {\n    constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;\n    constexpr size_t colBlock = 128;\n    for (size_t ii = 0;                             int rank, int size, const int dims[2]) {\n    std::vector<double> result;\n    std::vector<double> tile;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        result.resize(N * N);\n
